- **continue**: `/continue` is the **orchestrator**, not a direct implementation command. It reads project state, determines the next action, and dispatches to the appropriate phase command (e.g., `/compass`, `/implement`, `/test`). At Phase 6 it delegates to `/implement` — it does not duplicate `/implement`'s behavior. Its unique value is **state management, phase advancement, bug-routing, and next-action select ([josiahH-cf/orchestratr](https://github.com/josiahH-cf/orchestratr/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/continue.md))
